// Mirror Image variants that actually tick down and clean up on combat end.
// Vanilla MIRROR_IMAGE_* has no TickType so duration is ignored, status sticks
// until OnAttacked+IsMiss or Long Rest. Used by Lazurite Eye and Elven Shield.
new entry "AMP_MIRROR_IMAGE_1"
type "StatusData"
data "StatusType" "BOOST"
using "MIRROR_IMAGE_1"
data "StackId" "AMP_MIRROR_IMAGE_1"
data "TickType" "EndTurn"
data "RemoveEvents" "OnAttacked;OnCombatEnded"
